SELECT /*+  NO_MERGE  */ /* 2a.131 */
       x.*
       ,c.name con_name
  FROM cdb_advisor_executions x
       LEFT OUTER JOIN v$containers c ON c.con_id = x.con_id
ORDER BY
       x.con_id,
       x.owner, x.task_id;
